Title: Cultura e opulencia do Brasil por suas drogas, e minas, com varias noticias curiosas do modo de fazer o assucar; plantar, e beneficiar o tabaco; tirar ouro das minas; e descubrir as da prata
Creator: André João Antonil (title page)
Publisher: Lisboa: na Officina Real Deslandesiana (title page)
Publication Date: 1711 (title page: 'Anno de 1711')
Rights: Public Domain (pd-us-by-date: published 1711); the archive.org record carries no rights field, so the imprint date is the basis
Language: por
Source: archive.org item culturaeopulenci00anto, full text from the item's own djvu.txt. Fetched directly 2026-11-15 by the Library thread; the byte count of the downloaded file (395,916) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected. Rights basis: published 1711, out of US copyright by date. OCR quality: readable Portuguese with the long s read as f; accents and spaced capitals in headings are garbled. Word count of the host text: 62,040. Line numbers cited in this header are lines of the host OCR; add 12 for this file's own line numbers.
Content note: Four parts. Dedication to the mill owners at OCR lines 107-182; preface at 216-285; licences (1710-1711) at 296-424. Part I, sugar, at 187 and from chapter I at 442; Part II, tobacco, at 4965; Part III, the gold mines, at 5910; Part IV, cattle and royal contracts, at 8260; text ends at 8754. Part I, chapter IX, how the mill owner should deal with his slaves, at 1318-1579; the chaplain's duty to teach the household and slaves in chapter IV at about 862-882. Index at 8783-9192.
